[Sanctuary of Eyridane and Morana]
The small path ends as the trees part, revealing a small clearing. Sunlight filters down from above, splashing the vibrant green grass and wildflowers. The noise of the central part of the grove all but fades away in this quiet sanctuary. An altar of stone rests in the center of the small clearing of verdant grass. Tangled vines have grown wild across the hard stone and though no markings can be seen, it is obvious that this is a place sacred to the twin goddesses of nature. Overhead the soft warbling of songbirds blends with the chirping crickets below into a sublime symphony of sounds. Small trinkets of adoration and worship have been left at the base of the altar, each one a symbol of devotion to their beloved gods.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Harru the Galirrim,  Karrl the Galirrim,  Kynea the Zacotl (sitting down),  Eolei the Silvarin,  Arik the Silvarin (sitting down),  Bjeorn the Galirrim,  Maibe the Varanus's corpse (laying on the ground)
In this room are: simple stone altar
Obvious exits are: west, an arch of twisting branches

The 1st fox squirrel just wandered east.

rel

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. [Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

[Merchant Square]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained square. Through the open facade, young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small, but functional sign reads, "Malinar's Magic and Mayhem".

[Alerio Pass]
The northern gate of Urda leads directly into a neat and well kept stone pathway that gently inclines upwards. To either side of you are high walls, behind which the voices of Urda's rich and well educated can be heard softly. Numerous workers, travelers and wagons trundle along this road, as it is the main path between the center of Urda and the two northern wings of the city. [Temple of the Eyranas]
Amazingly the interior of the temple is teaming with life. Birds fly freely amongst the tall trees and shrubs which give the appearance of the outside instead of in. Small animals scurry across the ground in an almost permanent state of play. A large fountain set in the center of the area spouts water high into the air which then settles back into the fountain without even the smallest splash. Statues of the Goddess's lie scattered among the various ponds and gardens of the temple. A small grassy pasture free of any growth takes its place on the northeastern side of the temple which sits beneath a beautiful green, moss covered altar. A gaggle of bushes trimmed to portray letters spells out the word "Life" in the northwestern corner of the temple. A small path that leads through some thick overgrowth to the west provides passage to another room. To the south an overgrown arch provides exit back to the street.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

Through the open facade, young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge. [Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

[Merchant Square]
An eye-catching black striped tiger is emblazoned on the facade of lacquered wooden building on the south side of the square. Its shoulders raised high, and face frozen in a fierce roar. Through the many, high-arched, steel frame windows, a wooden figure can be seen wearing the latest gentleman's fashion. Grey smoke rises from a single stone chimney. A simple plaque hanging from a twisted wrought iron rod reads, "Regal Fashion". Smooth stone steps lead to the entrance. On the east side of the street a large brick chimney funnels white billowy smoke into the air projecting the aroma of fresh baked goods into the area. Through the large bay window a burly human man can be seen kneading and forming dough and placing his creations into an oven. The heavy mahogany door is propped seemingly propped to allow for relief from the ovens intense heat. [Merchant Square]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained square. Through the open facade, young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small, but functional sign reads, "Malinar's Magic and Mayhem".

The area is illuminated.

In this room are: northern monument
Obvious exits are: west, southwest, south, southeast, east, north, portal, open door

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

[Secluded Grove]

A small group of sturdy Galirrim has gathered around a small spring of crystal clear water. The area is illuminated.

[Sanctuary of Eyridane and Morana]
The small path ends as the trees part, revealing a small clearing. Sunlight filters down from above, splashing the vibrant green grass and wildflowers. The noise of the central part of the grove all but fades away in this quiet sanctuary. An altar of stone rests in the center of the small clearing of verdant grass. Tangled vines have grown wild across the hard stone and though no markings can be seen, it is obvious that this is a place sacred to the twin goddesses of nature. Overhead the soft warbling of songbirds blends with the chirping crickets below into a sublime symphony of sounds. Small trinkets of adoration and worship have been left at the base of the altar, each one a symbol of devotion to their beloved gods.

The area is illuminated.

In this room are: simple stone altar
Obvious exits are: west, an arch of twisting branches

[Travers Town Square]
Small marble benches are scattered neatly about the square welcoming travelers to take a seat or to possibly empty pebbles from their boots. Cobblestone streets decorate the square alternating from browns to reds in a random pattern. Small statues of the world's races stand behind each bench as if standing sentinel. Tall lamp posts with wrought iron lanterns look down on the neat and clean streets. An occasional waste engineer wanders through, sweeping up dirt and trash into tin dustpans. Tucked away in a corner is an old door leading to a rundown store. The loud echoing of dripping water can be heard coming from a metal grate which is recessed into the street.
